2026-10-31T04:04:02.6588049Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-31T04:04:02.6614713Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 11 with the fused QKV producer behind its 12-GPU in-job gate

Round 11 changes one thing against round 10 (41150387984, 9896.09): the
PP4 config sets prefill.qkv_fused_gate, so the job runs
scripts/select-firmus-prefill-qkv.py on all twelve Prefill GPUs before
model load. The gate compares the fused QKV GEMM epilogue (RMSNorm, NeoX
RoPE and FP8 written straight from the FP32 accumulators) with the
serving path, stock DeepGEMM 1D2D QKV plus the fp32 post kernel, at
M 3994 and 7988 with the model's own q_norm/k_norm weights.

Adoption requires, on every GPU: no worse max/mean abs error and FP8
code mismatch than fp32 against an FP64 reference for all five outputs
(Q, contiguous K/V, paged K/V caches), the same after 300 back-to-back
fused calls, and fused faster than GEMM plus post by at least 1 percent
at M 7988 in both timing orders. Otherwise, or on any timeout or error,
SGLANG_PREFILL_QKNORM stays fp32 and the run is a round-10 repeat.

Why: the post kernel re-reads the 147 MB BF16 QKV that the GEMM wrote,
about 81 us per layer. Removing that round trip is expected to cut
45-60 us per layer, 1.1-1.4 ms of the 115-118 ms PP0 period, about
+1 percent, which is the gap to 10k. The Genkai H100 probe
(41160677437, PJM 7220768) is still queued, so this H200 gate is the
first speed measurement, and it decides adoption by itself.

No build: the fused kernel is a runtime SGLang overlay already in
sglang_overlay_patches, and the image stays the pinned e7b09add
(41150387984). Everything else, including topology, c232, Decode cap 48
and every other kernel pin, is unchanged.

The gate test now asserts the flag is on in this config; the sbatch
default remains off.
